- Derived `Delta(r)=diag(r)*off(r)^2` exactly, including diagonal linear factors and degree `2D^2-D`; shorter target-support `Q(r)=H(r)*diag(r)*off(r)` has degree `D^2+D` and captures nonidentity signed four-sum support but not multiplicities/certified witnesses. Proved `M_ij=S3(u_i,u_j,r)` has rank at most three, making the proposed determinant zero for every target once D>=4, including empty targets. Thus it is **not** a valid fast membership predicate. Dense-output costs are Omega(D²) coefficient slots for this representation, not general indexing lower bounds. [pcc:unverified]
- Established a sharper uniform-target bound by counting signed four-multisets up to negation. For inherited B31 and order2147446831, at most338272 nonidentity target abscissae are reachable among1073723415; at least99.96849542486693% of uniform nonidentity blind targets are misses. This improves the earlier loose ordered-tuple counting bound under **the same exact signed-base model**, without measuring useful rank or runtime. [pcc:unverified]
- After publishing the math source at `b1d3da5dbcd0b21f5616060df314e22c5fec59bc`, performed local SymPy symbolic-polynomial residual checks for off-diagonal factorization, diagonal tangent identity and coefficient-pencil equality; all three residuals were exactly zero on retry. This is neither source-frozen native Rust, independent PCC signing, Lean verification nor a performance measurement. Formula/procedure and failed first attempt are preserved in `iteration10/VALIDATION.md`.
- Parallel independent GitHub source/status reads were used by one coordinator; no native agent worker launcher was exposed. No Rust, validation or algebra subagent ran, no separately attested worker result or elapsed-time gain. Rust implementation was **not** launched because the discriminating candidate remains missing; only independent test preparation was made.

### Decisions made (and why)

- Fallback literature and source checks: existing repo baseline audit (APS cold, generic Run72/75, maintained IC/rho), Semaev 2004, Petit–Kosters–Messeng 2016, and RR5 `native/relation_adapter.rs` blob `ae4fca2ca6e95304b8473a821bd75419c7d1d267`. No new novelty asserted; shared KB MCP and Conductor are not exposed.
- Added `research/cheap_ecdlp_cold_gate_20261123/src/bin/rr5_receipt_check.rs`: independent read-only original-curve mixed-RHS checker, with `[c,-b|a]` rows, exact Gauss-Jordan augmented target uniqueness, original-arithmetic `[d]G=Q` replay, authenticated/unchecked prefix accounting, malformed input guards and 11 new authored tests. Original code commit succeeded even though its GitHub tool call returned ReadTimeout; a readback confirmed file blob `331170dd59fdb726a83c58cf54369ad37656c4f8`. A follow-up safety commit `f094aa75f81205a010f5c817256a12d46400d4dd` fixed noncanonical coordinates and i64::MIN malformed signed multiplicities; final blob `44f4cd3807358651951b7493743237a4813e1784`.
- Committed public fixture `public_rr5_sample.json` at `a512874749bace4dccaf2d7a75621a50be77b9c1`, a **synthetic** Prime31 group equation, not extracted or blind. Python mathematical group oracle checked four base points, Q=(281707092,1865416923)=[5]G, [n]G=O and P0=[n-4]G+Q. A disjoint Python F101 rank oracle checked 2,500 synthetic consistent augmented streams and 28,343 prefixes against an independently written model of the Gauss-Jordan pivot criterion; all matched, with 2,153 determined and 2,050 determined before the end. This did not run Rust and is not signed independent validation. [pcc:unverified]
- Appended the scope, source identities, CLI commands, partial accounting, negative-control retention and next admission dependencies to `research/cheap_ecdlp_cold_gate_20261123/README.md` in commit `e04a4911a5f93841f6fef660240bc53185e292d9`. Native `cargo`/`rustc` are absent (`command -v` yielded no path). No cloud/CI job, reserved compute, native compile/test or complete cold benchmark was initiated.

### Decisions made (and why)

- One coordinator and **zero actual native worker slots**. Logical lead/baseline package reviewed original baselines and nonoverlapping target-pivot admission hypothesis; Rust integration package authored only the isolated consumer; validation package prepared independent group/rank math checks and poison cases. These packages were coordinator-executed with concurrent independent GitHub read calls, not spawned independent agents or external signed validators; no elapsed-time gain is claimed.
- The new mixed-RHS checker intentionally does not reinterpret `aG+bQ` as G-only known RHS, does not call the neighboring RR5 producer and does not accept a hidden scalar. Only the last augmented target-coordinate pivot can recover Q, with full original-arithmetic replay. All naturally missing relations, rejected/timeout extractor branches and paid compute constraints remain out of scope.
- Preserve the earlier APS/BSGS cold loss, generic 1024Q cohort limitations, Run72/Run75 negative evidence and source-specific rho comparisons. No new speedup or working blind solver is claimed.

### Open threads (unfinished; next)

- Source-exact Rust 1.100.0 `cargo test --manifest-path research/cheap_ecdlp_cold_gate_20261123/Cargo.toml --bin rr5_receipt_check --release` and CLI fixture replay on a permitted noncompeting runner; freeze Cargo.lock, binary hash, raw stdout/stderr, compiler and dep identities. The checker presently has **11 unexecuted authored Rust tests**, separate from the original binary's 15 unexecuted tests.
- Read-only ingest the owning RR5/RR9 lane's frozen **natural public unknown-index** signed tuples plus Q and factor base; independently replay authentic group equations, exact useful augmented rank, first target pivot, scalar recovery and all rejected/censored attempts. Compare fully charged same-host cold T=1 to strongest maintained IC, optimized WZ negation-rho and cold BSGS, and report other T cohorts separately. No new benchmark reservation or source rewrite before owner/host admission.

### Errors (what went wrong, how it was handled)

- The first GitHub create_file request returned `UNKNOWN ReadTimeout` despite a successful remote commit; fetched exact blob and PR head before resuming rather than duplicating the file. No missing data was concealed.
- Native agent launcher, Conductor, shared KB MCP, local Rust toolchain and admitted isolated lab route remain unavailable. The Python checks are development-only, not compiled program evidence, independent signed PCC or true Prime31 index-calculus recovery. [pcc:unverified]
